What git cannot show: **gh#190's owner ruling named the WRONG FILE** — the probe reads the button PlayExit.astro owns, and that file imports only the CONSTANT from `_arm-gate` while running its own timer, so a fix confined to the named file could not have moved the stalled run; counting every file with its own `setTimeout` on that constant found FOUR (`_arm-gate.ts`, `PlayExit.astro`, `PlayerSetup.astro` twice, `siamsi.ts`), and the cure went to the class · the defect was live on **10 of 11 routes** against a real served `dist/`, not the one route the ticket documents · `siamsi.ts` REVERTED out and filed gh#200 — `DeviceMotionEvent.timeStamp` is assigned at renderer-create time on the main thread, so during a stall it IS handler time and the cure is inert there; the version briefly committed carried a comment asserting the opposite · REFUTE r1 found the window short by the PRESS DURATION (anchored to pointerdown, timer starts at render after pointerup) and **the probe is structurally blind to it** — it synthesises the transition tap as touchStart immediately followed by touchEnd, so that gap is zero-width in every leg it can run; cure is seeding from pointerup too and taking the later stamp · r1 also found the seed path had NO automated red anywhere (no `document` in node; the stall leg is refused under the CI tag by design) · r2 found the new fake `document` ignored the capture argument — the one load-bearing registration property, since PlayerSetup's swallow stops propagation in capture · `short-stick` was SKIP/EXEMPT in BOTH probe legs, so it is **unmeasured, not clean**, and no green covers it (gh#199) · `npx tsc --noEmit` does not typecheck `.astro` at all, so it was never evidence for two of three changed files — `npx astro check` is · `bundle-freeze-check` passes at +3.8% of a ±5% budget, so the next addition of this size in one batch reds it · the gate suite must run ALONE (runbook): a green taken beside a sibling's Chrome is not evidence, and distinct ports only suppress the collision error.
dec: owner 2026-09-04 via OPT, 4 rulings, each on its own ticket and cited not restated — gh#192 (g) delete the clause write no replacement · gh#192 (f) agent fixes the 21px footer to 44px now, focus ring takes a default · ADR-0040 Thai class noun → agent proposes 3-5 candidate words, owner picks (gh#201) · next focus = browser wave + gh#192 (b) + gh#199, gh#9 NOT selected. ### S2026-09-01#2
done: 2 batches PUSHED — `7390261` (18 files, gh#172/#185/#188/#189) and `a2abeca` (16 files, gh#182 + play-exit diagnostics). Batch 1's CI went RED then GREEN on a re-run of the byte-identical commit, and that green deployed. gh#172 gh#185 gh#188 gh#189 CLOSED with evidence, 41 → 37 open. What git cannot show: **the first fix for the arm window was worse than the bug it fixed.** A construction-time read of `disabled` cannot tell caller intent from the gate's own disable-all residue, so cancelling a gate mid-window and re-arming the same nodes stranded every control including close, with reload the only exit — reachable through the open-then-reopen pattern on two header-triggered modals where the trigger keeps focus. The pre-merge REFUTE caught it with an executed repro; I reproduced it independently one variable apart, and again after the fix to confirm the same harness heals. **The implementing agent overruled my chosen fix form and was right**: my restore-on-cancel would have re-enabled a control the arm hook had just deliberately disabled, because the disarmer is normally called AFTER the window closes — it would have reintroduced the original bug on that path and needed a second flag, making it the larger diff too. **The shipped edit-players test was GREEN while the bug was live, and two of the eleven routes were never executed by it at all** — its import rewrite emitted invalid JavaScript for a default-plus-named import and ignored type-only imports. **gh#182's "ten routes scroll" was too high**: the fit probe files any element whose own overflow is not hidden as a scroller, and visible overflow is not one, so one route's 9px was never reachable content. That same probe **flaps by ~27px** — one route read 160/187/160 across three runs on two builds it never touched, so every small delta in the earlier analysis is noise. **cursed-number cannot reach zero at 320**: worst screen measures 2.21 viewports, and every removable block sums to 252px against 689px. Its history panel is not even on the overflowing screen, so the ruling to keep it open never constrained the problem. **The play-exit red was neither code nor environment** — one run, attempt 1 red and attempt 2 green, same image and same Chrome; and CI had been generating the diagnostic JSON and discarding it, because no upload-artifact step existed anywhere in the workflow. A sibling agent destroyed another's committed-nowhere work by restoring from a copy taken before that work landed; my brief demanded calibration while forbidding the mutant it requires.
dec: owner rulings 2026-09-01 — gh#189 the ban is scoped to PLAYER IDENTITY, counters explicitly allowed, so the scan is not widened and its scope is written down · gh#182 an inner self-scroller does NOT violate the criterion, so two routes get pinned as fitting · gh#182 keep the roster strip, cut the odds pill · gh#182 keep cursed-number's history open · **gh#182 cursed-number at 320x568 is an ACCEPTED EXCEPTION on the stated reason, every other route at 320 still expected to fit** · zero-trigger's speed hint may go with the box that carried it · 2 src pushes approved, the second conditioned on gates green · find the play-exit runner cause now rather than filing it · continue rather than saving, then save. Mine, logged: shipped the WeakSet the agent argued for over my own smaller fix, because being right outranks being small · reverted a floor removal that measured zero gain and would have unpinned a header, rather than keeping a cosmetic regression for nothing · did NOT file the two follow-up tickets despite the work being done, because the SH grant covers closing verified-done work and not opening new tickets — they sit in next: instead · left two gh#182 boxes unticked because they are genuinely unmet, and said so in the ticket · treated the diagnostics as the answer to the flake rather than any tolerance change, since raising the arm delay would widen the real guard until a deliberate second contact exits.

### S2026-09-01#1
done: 6 commits PUSHED in 2 batches — `0fbdcbb` `04fcde5` `354e5ff` (CI 33465236171) then `08be978` `6e965cf` `ff1f22b` (CI 33467799692), both success incl. the deploy step. What git cannot show: **the inherited gh#187 picture was wrong in a load-bearing way** — on the 8 unfixed routes the CONFIRM branch already re-armed; only close/cancel plus the rules/help/avatar closers were bare, so the same dialog shipped guarded on one button and open on the two beside it. Each route's `arm-reveal-paths` test enumerates reveal RECEIVERS (`innerHTML` / `style.display` writes), and a closer that only hides a modal writes neither — the class was invisible to the instrument built to find it, BY CONSTRUCTION not by oversight. Same shape in gh#185: `play-exit`'s test built its set from directories owning a roster bridge and asserted a floor of nine against exactly nine, so timebomb and dice-loser sat outside the loop by construction. **F1's premise was STALE before I started** — the play-screen-fit pair had already run green on a real Linux runner in CI 33405521676, so only the probe's own red direction was owed; and box 5 looked unsatisfiable because the file's header says scroll is "REPORTED, never asserted", until the inverted `FITS_ROWS` guard turned out to assert exactly the pinned rows. **Four measured corrections to claims I had accepted**: the markup numbered defaults were NOT player-visible (every node sits in a container hidden until its script write, so severity was overstated and I had already repeated it); `how-close-is-near` DOES use the shared cast, via its own seat helper routing through `mascotNames`; the comment-stripper count is 14 implementations across 11 files with 8 fail-open, not 7/11 at implementation granularity; and only gh#173 of seven audited tickets was fully provable. **Two instrument bugs found, neither by a gate**: timebomb's boom readback returned one frame before the explosion painted, so under reduced motion it captured the ticking image byte-identical to the ticking read — a real explosion and a dead renderer looked the same, and full motion hid it; and the gh#179 calibration evidence was written as `.log`, which this repo's gitignore drops, so committed proof would have silently not existed. The markup numbered-default scan also knew only Arabic digits — a Thai-numeral default would have walked through a green guard in a Thai-first product.

### S2026-08-29#2
done: **มือลั่น + ยิงธง ARE LIVE** — merged `27c9919` to main, deploy run 33238030132 success, verified against the running site: `/c/party/` lists 5 games (was 3), `/game/freeze-tap/` and `/game/cannon-flag/` both 200, `/game/power-meter/` still 404 as intended. **The browser pass is what earned that merge**: cannon-flag passed 14 unit tests, `npm run ci`, `astro check` and all 20 CI probes while two of its three core controls did NOTHING on a phone — `releaseCharge` was bound to WINDOW and called `preventDefault()` on every page-wide `touchend` BEFORE its guard, suppressing the synthetic click Chrome builds from touchend and killing both angle steppers and the sound toggle (`f0e090f`; measured 58.0° through three real taps on an enabled 44x44 control, 65→68° after). Also: power-meter's 3 defects + the auto-lock bug the curve fix exposed (`0cbf38c`), three fake-DOM holes closed across two test files, `#cf-sound-toggle` 32→44px (then `flex: none`, it measured back 43 as a shrunk flex child), `scripts/driver.mjs` gains `session.hold()`. Unmerged on `fix/gate-visibility-and-ci-cost`: cannon-flag's 3 screen builders renamed to `render*` so `arm-gate-coverage-check` can see them (`07b5dd9`), and ci.yml concurrency + probes-only-when-dist-can-differ (`70991a1`).
dec: **A fake `addEventListener() {}` stub is a hole, not a fake** — power-meter's `matchMedia` and cannon-flag's `window` both accepted listeners and never called them, which is exactly why 14 tests and 20 probes passed a game whose buttons were dead; `innerHTML` as a raw string hid an `<a href>` in `EMPTY_GAUGE_SVG` from the anchor sweep (a LIVE ADR-0014 hole behind a green suite); `clientWidth: 0` made the whole burst path unreachable. Every new assertion this session was planted-and-reddened before being trusted, and TWO were thrown out for failing on fixed and broken code alike — a test that reds on both is a broken measuring tool, not a finding. **Owner rulings:** §7 governs whether an effect EXISTS and §10 how it LOOKS (both infinite loops gone, all 16 effects kept, `animation: ... infinite` now 0); the shell-owned Thai copy is reviewed and approved as shipped; gh#123 resolves toward the gate, so `CLAUDE.md`'s domain-term allowance is `.md`-only. **CI cost, measured on run 33236941662: browser probes are 698s of 794s = 88%**, every other step totals 96s; 7 runs were triggered this session where 2 were needed. Rejected after measuring: splitting probes into a parallel job — ci.yml's own comment says a separate job must rebuild `dist/`, and a probe measuring a regenerated `dist/` is not measuring the bytes Deploy uploads.

Run-level, throwaway branch, one variable apart: stale `bb25788` run 32708885792 -> annotation PRESENT, fresh `64c2a0d` run 32708951665 -> ABSENT, BOTH GREEN — conclusion cannot distinguish the legs, only the annotation carries signal; read via the check-runs annotations API, never the run summary · **gh#72 closed with 2 of its 3 boxes WITHDRAWN, evidenced on the ticket, not silently dropped** — five strippers exist not four (`arm-gate-coverage-check.mjs` landed in `dbd641f` before filing), three semantic families, only checkpoint+stable-exit congruent and only merge-upward is safe; the merge was refused on the OWNERSHIP answer: comment grammar is language-owned, so one shared incomplete stripper makes one ceiling load-bearing in five gates with five different preconditions · what shipped instead is a per-gate pin in `leave-confirm-check.mjs` and `arm-gate-coverage-check.mjs` that measures each gate's OWN scan set and reds on a `//` inside a quoted value, calibrated three ways per gate (planted hazard, clean tree, stubbed detector) · **box 3 REVERTED after implementation** — pointing the selftest's `shipped` at the live config turned `assert.equal(hashCsp(shipped), PAIRS_HASH)` into a hard fail on any real CSP edit, reversing the 2026-08-23 warn ruling, and CI's `--selftest && <scan>` would ALSO short-circuit the scan that does the warning; separately it made six `.replace()`-derived fixtures silent no-ops once their anchors drift · **a survey of all 13 CI-invoked gates found NO other instance of that class**, but turned the check back on this session's own new text and found its ceiling disclosure wrong on 2 of 3 legs, fixed in `72b9684`

### S2026-08-15#1

### S2026-08-13#5

done: scaffold #13 → `02708ed` push แล้ว · **CI เขียวครบรอบแรก** deploy skip ถูกเพราะยังไม่มี secret · **DoD #13 ข้อ 1-3 ปิด เหลือข้อ 4** · REFUTE 1 รอบ 6 findings แก้ครบ · map #1 ลิงก์ตาย 14 → 0 · **ผลรันจริง + แก้มติ 9 ข้อ + ของที่รู้แต่ยังไม่แก้ →** [#13 comment](https://github.com/warischa/watduang/issues/13#issuecomment-5278598792)

dec: why ทุกข้ออยู่ในคอมเมนต์นั้น — `build.format=directory` + `trailingSlash=always` · manifest static import ตอน build ส่วน island รับ `id` ผ่าน `data-game-id` แล้ว `import.meta.glob` · CSP header-only และไฟล์ต้องอยู่ `public/staticwebapp.config.json` (ที่รากไม่ถึง prod) · pin wildcard Google ไม่ไล่ exact host · `script-src` ไม่มี `unsafe-inline` → snippet AdSense ต้อง external · `ads===false` บังคับ · field ใหม่ `tagline` · ไม่อัป astro แม้ audit เตือน

⚠ รูป OG อย่าใช้ Pillow — เครื่องนี้ไม่มี libraqm สระไทยกลายเป็นวงกลมจุดทั้งที่ draw สำเร็จ → `node scripts/make-og.mjs <id>` แล้วเปิดดูด้วยตา
